# Seed 66 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - islands again: 3.6% ahead of seed 64 at generation 146 - the default from here (Iteration 454)

`seed 66 12h widths noevolved islands then experiment footprint.py ... then experiment review-checks.py spread` at
94e6742bb (Iteration 450): the second islands run, the after-seed-65 batch's first (the deferred gate reading, I+ with its
fuser) and the first freeze's (sharedcall, tail, kstack, tailpn off). The evolution 03:39:24; it evolved 11 h 16 min.
45,676 designs, generations 0-178: 33,071 alive, 12,605 screened (28.3%), none died.

**No verdict** - no comparison step. **Islands, the second time, against seed 64 at generation 146** (seed 64's database
kept): fastest **0.1208 against 0.1253 - 3.6% ahead**; smallest 14,045 against 14,047; best Q -6.7%; hypervolume +1.8%;
at 32 bits level (0.3326 each). Seed 65 was 6.8% ahead there. Both runs met the trial's two conditions - **islands the
default from seed 67** (Iteration 449's rule). **The freeze, against seed 65 at generation 90** (the chain's spread over
seeds 62-66): fastest 0.1286 against 0.1288, smallest 14,077 against 14,063 (+0.1%) - no loss. By its end: fastest 0.121,
smallest 14,037 bytes.

**Facts.** Its front: 149 designs, 64 with the JIT; tos on 92. heldout (sieve): Spearman 0.985, the fastest tenth 0.815,
the pick's regret +1.4%. **Fast-end optimism 1.110 - the deferred gate reading did not lower it** (seed 65: 1.115). No
32-bit failure. I+: 39 front designs with rtiplus, all 39 with the fuser now - 38 of the 85 plain designs (45%). The gene
votes over seeds 64-66: **candidates alignhot, jithelp, jitre** (12 of 12). The footprint check (its 16 fastest): Spearman
0.524 at 100 words, **0.206 at 10,000** - the top reordering on large programs this time (seed 64: 0.888, seed 65: 0.550).
Its chain's remote checks, of seed 65's front: results/xcheck-seed66.md.

---
# Evolved VM designs

33071 designs evaluated, 33071 alive, 12605 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): 854ef1167c, speed 0.131, binary + image 29,476, memory 168 KB - Q 0.197 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
